### [1] [The impact of multi-agent debate protocols on debate quality: a controlled case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28813)
*Ramtin Zargari Marandi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究首次系统性地解耦了多智能体辩论（MAR）中协议效应与模型效应，发现辩论协议设计对系统性能有显著影响：新型RA-CR协议在收敛速度上优于传统CR协议，WR协议产生更多对等引用，NI基准保留最大论点多样性，揭示了交互性与收敛性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体辩论研究存在方法论缺陷：辩论协议（如智能体数量、轮数、聚合规则）通常保持固定，仅变化模型相关因素，导致难以区分协议效应与模型效应的影响程度。

Method: 采用控制变量法，在宏观经济案例研究中（20个多样化事件、五种随机种子、匹配的提示词/解码策略），对比四种协议：回合内协议（WR）、跨回合协议（CR）、新型排名自适应跨回合协议（RA-CR）以及无交互基准（NI）。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1) RA-CR收敛速度优于CR；(2) WR产生更高频率的对等引用；(3) NI保留最大论点多样性，且该指标在各主要协议间保持稳定；(4) 交互性与收敛性之间存在权衡关系。

Conclusion: 辩论协议设计对系统性能具有显著影响。当以达成共识为首要目标时，RA-CR协议表现最优。该研究为多智能体辩论系统的协议选择提供了实证依据。

Abstract: In multi-agent debate (MAD) systems, performance gains are often reported; however, because the debate protocol (e.g., number of agents, rounds, and aggregation rule) is typically held fixed while model-related factors vary, it is difficult to disentangle protocol effects from model effects. To isolate these effects, we compare three main protocols, Within-Round (WR; agents see only current-round contributions), Cross-Round (CR; full prior-round context), and novel Rank-Adaptive Cross-Round (RA-CR; dynamically reorders agents and silences one per round via an external judge model), against a No-Interaction baseline (NI; independent responses without peer visibility). In a controlled macroeconomic case study (20 diverse events, five random seeds, matched prompts/decoding), RA-CR achieves faster convergence than CR, WR shows higher peer-referencing, and NI maximizes Argument Diversity (unaffected across the main protocols). These results reveal a trade-off between interaction (peer-referencing rate) and convergence (consensus formation), confirming protocol design matters. When consensus is prioritized, RA-CR outperforms the others.

</details>


### [2] [An Empirical Study of Multi-Agent Collaboration for Automated Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29632)
*Yang Shen,Zhenyi Yi,Ziyi Zhao,Lijun Sun,Dongyang Li,Chin-Teng Lin,Yuhui Shi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多智能体系统在自动化机器学习优化中的协作架构，对比了单智能体基线、子智能体架构（并行探索后融合）与智能体团队架构（专家前执行交接）三种方案，发现子智能体模式适合快速宽泛搜索，智能体团队模式适合深度复杂重构，提倡根据任务复杂度动态路由的混合架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前社区正从单一大语言模型向多智能体系统演进，但多智能体协作的最优框架尚未被充分探索。作者旨在通过严格的实证研究，为自主研究系统的设计提供理论依据和实践指南。

Method: 采用严格控制的基于执行结果的测试平台，集成Git worktree隔离机制和显式全局记忆；在固定时间预算下，对单智能体基线、子智能体架构（并行探索+事后整合）和智能体团队架构（专家预执行交接）进行了系统对比实验。

Result: 研究发现两种多智能体范式存在根本性权衡：子智能体模式作为高吞吐量搜索引擎，在严格时间约束下对宽泛浅层优化任务表现最优；智能体团队拓扑因多作者代码生成导致操作脆弱性较高，但在延长计算预算下能实现复杂架构重构所需的深度理论对齐。

Conclusion: 为未来自主研究系统设计提供可操作指南：推荐动态路由架构，根据实时任务复杂度自适应调整协作结构，而非采用单一固定模式。

Abstract: As AI agents evolve, the community is rapidly shifting from single Large Language Models (LLMs) to Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) to overcome cognitive bottlenecks in automated research. However, the optimal multi-agent coordination framework for these autonomous agents remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we present a systematic empirical study investigating the comparative efficacy of distinct multi-agent structures for automated machine learning optimization. Utilizing a rigorously controlled, execution-based testbed equipped with Git worktree isolation and explicit global memory, we benchmark a single-agent baseline against two multi-agent paradigms: a subagent architecture (parallel exploration with post-hoc consolidation) and an agent team architecture (experts with pre-execution handoffs). By evaluating these systems under strictly fixed computational time budgets, our findings reveal a fundamental trade-off between operational stability and theoretical deliberation. The subagent mode functions as a highly resilient, high-throughput search engine optimal for broad, shallow optimizations under strict time constraints. Conversely, the agent team topology exhibits higher operational fragility due to multi-author code generation but achieves the deep theoretical alignment necessary for complex architectural refactoring given extended compute budgets. These empirical insights provide actionable guidelines for designing future autoresearch systems, advocating for dynamically routed architectures that adapt their collaborative structures to real-time task complexity.

</details>

### [3] [StepCache: Step-Level Reuse with Lightweight Verification and Selective Patching for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28795)
*Azam Nouri*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: StepCache 提出一种后端无关的“步级（step-level）”缓存复用层：把 LLM 输出切分为有序步骤，检索最佳匹配的历史请求，用轻量任务感知校验器逐 step 验证，仅对失败片段做连续块选择性重生成（patching），并在语义变更时保守地 skip-reuse 回退到全量重生成；对 JSON 提供严格结构化输出强制（单步提取、必需键约束、一次性修复），对线性方程提供有界修复环 + 确定性兜底以保证正确性。CPU 微基准（222 请求/种子，3 种子）上平均延迟 2.13s→0.67s，中位 2.42s→0.01s，p95 3.38s→3.30s，总 token 36.1k→27.3k，端到端正确率 72.5%→100%；79.7% 走纯复用快路径，5.4% 打补丁，14.9% skip-reuse。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 服务中大量请求结构相似但存在局部差异（输出 schema、变量名、数值常量、必需键等）。现有语义缓存以整条响应为复用单元，属于全有或全无：局部变化时结果错误，全量重生成又重复计算大量可复用内容；KV/前缀缓存依赖特定模型、tokenizer 与上下文，且只加速重复前缀而非答案局部片段。论文希望引入介于两者之间的中间粒度复用单元，在保留正确性同时支持局部再生成。此外，可廉价验证的任务（JSON 解析、线性方程一致性）与开放式生成在验证强度上存在落差，需要可回退的保守策略。

Method: 1) 缓存单元为有序步骤序列：默认按段落、显式编号、列表分隔符启发式切分；JSON 任务强制单步分割（剥离代码围栏、抽取首个合法 JSON 对象/数组）。每条缓存记录包含 prompt 嵌入、步骤文本、约束元数据（任务类型、required_keys）、可选工具输出、溯源与计时信号。2) 检索：用 SentenceTransformers all-MiniLM-L6-v2 计算嵌入，FAISS 近似最近邻取单一最佳匹配请求（不跨请求混合步骤），元数据存本地库。3) 验证：规则式任务感知校验器。数学（a·v+b=c）解析 (a,b,c,v)，计算 v*=(c−b)/a，检查最终赋值、中间等式 av=N（N≠c−b）、所写方程常量（av+b=N，N≠c）；首个不一致步骤 i 之后的整个后缀标记为失败以保持依赖。JSON 检查可解析性与必需键缺失。4) 选择性修补：数学做连续后缀块修补，prompt 中注入 math_state_hint (a,b,c,v,v*,c−b) 防止沿用陈旧常量；JSON 用严格“仅合法 JSON”提示 + schema 示例 + 必需键约束，失败后最多一次带错误反馈的修复。5) 保守 skip-reuse：数学中 (a,b,c,v) 状态变化、首个失败步骤为 1、或不一致比例≥0.5 时直接全量重生成（value_change 情形用 force_skip_reuse 标注以隔离语义变更）。6) 缝合与终检：拼接步骤后做任务级完整性检查（JSON 解析+必需键、数学解一致性），失败则至多一次任务特定修复并复检，数学仍失败则返回确定性解 “v=v*”。7) 实现为 OpenAI 兼容 API 前的轻量 Python 层，不触碰 KV 状态；后端为 Qwen2.5-3B，本地 CPU 推理；全程结构化日志与计数器（baseline/reuse/verify/patch/skip）。8) 基准：每任务 n=10 个基础 prompt、每扰动 k=3 个变体，含 low/med/high 改写、value_change、keys_change；两阶段协议（warmup 灌缓存 + 评估阶段），222 请求/种子，种子 42–44。

Result: 表 1（3 种子均值，±标准差）：平均延迟 2.13±0.04s → 0.67±0.04s（约 3.2×）；中位延迟 2.42±0.05s → 0.01±0.00s；p95 3.38±0.03s → 3.30±0.04s（尾部仍由慢路径主导，改善有限）；总 token 36.1±0.1k → 27.3±0.9k（约 −24%），单请求 162.7±0.6 → 123.0±4.0；质量通过率 72.5±0.5% → 100%；最终检查通过率 72.5±0.5% → 100%；结果分布 79.7±2.4% 纯复用 / 5.4% 修补 / 14.9±2.4% skip-reuse。表 2 分任务：json low/med/high 均 100% 复用、100% 正确，分别省 119/84/89 token；json keys_change 100% 走修补、正确率 100%、多耗 42 token；math low/med/high 96.7% 复用 + 3.3% skip、正确率 100%，token 节省 −17/19/18；math value_change 100% skip-reuse、正确率 100%、仅多耗 2 token（跳过复用避免无效修补）。

Conclusion: StepCache 证明：在“解决方案骨架稳定、扰动局部化”的工作负载下，步级复用 + 轻量任务感知验证 + 选择性块修补可在后端无关的前提下显著降低平均延迟与 token 消耗，并把基线因整响应复用失效导致的 72.5% 正确率提升至 100%；skip-reuse 策略使语义变更场景下复用不至于反而增加成本。局限与未来工作：验证器只覆盖线性方程与 JSON，开放式生成需 LLM 判官或更昂贵校验（可能退化为 skip-reuse）；评测为 CPU 小模型微基准、样本量小、无 GPU 吞吐/利用率指标与真实突发 trace；尚未做缓存完整性加固（对抗性相似、投毒）、访问控制，也未扩展到代码与工具增强 agent 流程。

Abstract: We address LLM serving workloads where repeated requests share a common solution structure but differ in localized constraints, such as output schema, variable names, or numeric constants. Prior caching approaches typically reuse either full responses (semantic caching) or model-internal KV/prefix states, which are respectively brittle under partial changes or tightly coupled to specific backends. We present StepCache, a backend-agnostic step-level reuse layer that segments outputs into ordered steps, retrieves the best-matching cached request, verifies steps using lightweight task-aware checks, and regenerates only failing regions via selective patching. StepCache additionally supports strict structured-output enforcement for JSON, including single-step extraction, required-key constraints, and one-shot repair, as well as conservative skip-reuse fallbacks for semantic changes. For linear equations, StepCache promotes verification into correction via a bounded repair loop with a deterministic fallback that guarantees correctness when the backend model fails.
  In a CPU-only perturbation-heavy micro-benchmark on math and JSON variants, averaged over three seeds, StepCache reduces mean latency from 2.13 s to 0.67 s, median latency from 2.42 s to 0.01 s, and p95 latency from 3.38 s to 3.30 s. It also reduces total token usage from 36.1k to 27.3k and improves end-to-end correctness from 72.5% to 100% under task-specific checks and a stitched-output integrity check. Across requests, 79.7% take the reuse-only fast path, 5.4% require patching, and 14.9% trigger skip-reuse.

</details>

### [4] [OneComp: One-Line Revolution for Generative AI Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28845)
*Yuma Ichikawa,Keiji Kimura,Akihiro Yoshida,Yudai Fujimoto,Hiroki Tokura,Yamato Arai,Yoshiyuki Ishii,Yusei Kawakami,Genki Shikada,Achille Jacquemond,Yoshihiko Fujisawa,Katsuki Fujisawa,Takumi Honda,Akira Sakai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OneComp是一个开源的模型压缩框架，可根据模型标识符和硬件资源自动进行混合精度量化和渐进式压缩，支持从层压缩到块压缩再到全局压缩的多阶段优化流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型部署面临内存占用、延迟和硬件成本三大瓶颈；后训练压缩虽能缓解这些问题，但实际应用中面临量化算法、精度预算、校准策略和硬件执行方案碎片化的困境，缺乏统一的、可复现的解决方案。

Method: OneComp框架包含三个核心模块：模型自动检查（给定模型ID和硬件信息）、混合精度分配规划、以及渐进式量化执行（层级压缩→块级细化→全局细化）。架构设计将首个量化检查点作为可部署的基准点，后续阶段在同一模型上持续改进。

Result: 将前沿压缩研究转化为可扩展、开源、硬件感知的生产级流水线，实现了算法创新与实际部署之间的桥梁。

Conclusion: OneComp将专家级工作流转化为可复现的、资源自适应的流程，为生产级模型部署提供了统一解决方案。

Abstract: Deploying foundation models is increasingly constrained by memory footprint, latency, and hardware costs. Post-training compression can mitigate these bottlenecks by reducing the precision of model parameters without significantly degrading performance; however, its practical implementation remains challenging as practitioners navigate a fragmented landscape of quantization algorithms, precision budgets, data-driven calibration strategies, and hardware-dependent execution regimes. We present OneComp, an open-source compression framework that transforms this expert workflow into a reproducible, resource-adaptive pipeline. Given a model identifier and available hardware, OneComp automatically inspects the model, plans mixed-precision assignments, and executes progressive quantization stages, ranging from layer-wise compression to block-wise refinement and global refinement. A key architectural choice is treating the first quantized checkpoint as a deployable pivot, ensuring that each subsequent stage improves the same model and that quality increases as more compute is invested. By converting state-of-the-art compression research into an extensible, open-source, hardware-aware pipeline, OneComp bridges the gap between algorithmic innovation and production-grade model deployment.

</details>


### [5] [Structural Pass Analysis in Football: Learning Pass Archetypes and Tactical Impact from Spatio-Temporal Tracking Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28916)
*Oktay Karakuş,Hasan Arkadaş*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于传球与防守结构交互分析足球传球的结构化框架。利用2022年FIFA世界杯的追踪和事件数据，推导出三个互补的结构指标（线路绕过得分、空间获取指标、结构破坏指数），并整合为战术影响值（TIV）。通过无监督聚类识别出四种传球原型：循环型、破坏型、破防线型和空间扩展型。研究表明，高TIV传球更可能导致进攻推进至进攻三区或禁区，同时揭示了球队和球员层面的结构性传球风格差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有传球评估方法主要依赖结果导向指标（如得分概率或控球价值），无法充分揭示传球如何影响对手的防守组织结构，缺乏对防守结构变化的有效量化分析。

Method: 利用同步追踪数据与事件数据，提取三个结构指标：线路绕过得分（Line Bypass Score）、空间获取指标（Space Gain Metric）和结构破坏指数（Structural Disruption Index），并整合为综合指标战术影响值（TIV）。采用无监督聚类方法对结构特征进行分类分析。

Result: 识别出四种可解释的传球原型（循环型、破坏型、破防线型、空间扩展型）；高TIV传球显著增加进入进攻三区和禁区的概率；不同球队展现出差异化的结构性传球风格；构建型后卫是结构推进的关键驱动因素；可识别出增强战术推进的结构性传球搭档关系。

Conclusion: 基于追踪数据的结构化表示方法能够揭示足球中可解释的战术模式，为分析球员、球队层面的结构性传球行为提供了有效框架。

Abstract: The increasing availability of spatio-temporal tracking data has created new opportunities for analysing tactical behaviour in football. However, many existing approaches evaluate passes primarily through outcome-based metrics such as scoring probability or possession value, providing limited insight into how passes influence the defensive organisation of the opponent. This paper introduces a structural framework for analysing football passes based on their interaction with defensive structure. Using synchronised tracking/event data, we derive three complementary structural metrics, Line Bypass Score, Space Gain Metric, and Structural Disruption Index, that quantify how passes alter the spatial configuration of defenders. These metrics are combined into a composite measure termed Tactical Impact Value (TIV), which captures the structural influence of individual passes. Using tracking and event data from the 2022 FIFA World Cup, we analyse structural passing behaviour across multiple tactical levels. Unsupervised clustering of structural features reveals four interpretable pass archetypes: circulatory, destabilising, line-breaking, and space-expanding passes. Empirical results show that passes with higher TIV are significantly more likely to lead to territorial progression, particularly entries into the final third and penalty box. Spatial, team-level analyses further reveal distinctive structural passing styles across teams, while player-level analysis highlights the role of build-up defenders as key drivers of structural progression. In addition, analysing passer-receiver interactions identifies structurally impactful passing partnerships that amplify tactical progression within teams. Overall, the proposed framework demonstrates how structural representations derived from tracking data can reveal interpretable tactical patterns in football.

</details>


### [6] [Beta-Scheduling: Momentum from Critical Damping as a Diagnostic and Correction Tool for Neural Network Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28921)
*Ivan Pasichnyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从阻尼谐振子模型（Qian, 1999）出发，把动量与学习率耦合为闭式调度 μ(t)=1-2√α(t)（"β-scheduling"，无新增可调超参）。在 ResNet-18/CIFAR-10 上五种子验证：达 90% 精度快 2.34×（p=4×10⁻⁴），但最终精度系统性低 0.46pp（p=0.009）；Hybrid-90（到达 90% 后切回 μ=0.9）以 95.45±0.05% 消除该代价且保持 2.4× 加速。诊断管线（仅在错分样本上做逐层梯度归因→外科式局部重训）具有跨优化器不变性（SGD 与 Adam 选出同样 3 层，100% 重合），但作者 v3 版穷举 35 种 3/7 层组合后发现：诊断选出的三元组仅排第 11/35（p=0.31），不优于随机选择；仅"顶层软信号"（含 layer3 的组合 +6.2 vs −1.4）和"底部避害信号"（低排名层重训致 −13~−20 净错误）成立。核心贡献不是精度提升，而是"临界阻尼调度 + 诚实的组合零假设"这一方法论工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准训练把动量（惯例 μ=0.9，可追溯到 1964 年）与学习率当作两个独立超参网格来调，缺乏理论依据。Qian (1999) 已证明带动量的 SGD 在连续时间极限下等价于受迫阻尼谐振子，最快收敛对应各特征模态的临界阻尼；作者主张"给定任意学习率调度 α(t)，动量轨迹应被完全确定"，即 μ(t)=1-2√α(t)（β-scheduling），除现有 LR 调度外零自由参数。由此产生三个待检验问题：(1) 该调度是否真的加速收敛，代价是什么；(2) 快早期/高终值之间的权衡能否被消除；(3) 早期版本（v2）声称"错分样本上的逐层梯度归因可定位应外科修复的层"，这一选择规则是否成立——若成立，可推广到 Adam 训练的 LLM 的"对齐税"缓解与模型修复。

Method: 1) 理论：由 μ(t)=1-2√α(t) 的振子推导（γ=1-μ, ω=√α，Δt=1、单位曲率；与精确离散最优 (1-√α)² 的关系为小 α 一阶近似），并讨论按特征模态 μ_k=1-2√(λ_k α) 的推广与谱依据（Olsen et al. 的权重奇异值 SDE）；给出三条可检验预言（最快早期收敛、无振荡、终值有竞争力）与对既有启发式的回溯解释（μ=0.9、1cycle 反比耦合、warmup）。实现上 μ 固定 clamp 到 [0.5, 0.99]（先验设定、从未调参，计为方法定义的一部分），高 LR 段因此略欠阻尼。
2) 实验设计：ResNet-18/CIFAR-10，200 epoch cosine LR 0.1→10⁻⁴，batch 128，wd 5e-4，5 个种子（42/123/456/789/1337），对照为基线 μ=0.9、1cycle（μ 线性 0.95→0.85→0.95）、Physics；统计检验用 Friedman、配对 t 检验、Wilcoxon（n=5 时 Wilcoxon 精确下限 p=0.031）；并做短调度对照（cosine 压缩到 T∈{60,100}）排除"调度长度混淆"。
3) 泛化：VGG-16(BN)（无跳跃连接）与 CIFAR-100（ResNet-18，用"基线终值 95%"作为相对里程碑，因 CIFAR-100 达不到 90%）。
4) 诊断管线五步：Scan（逐 epoch 阻尼区间分类：欠阻尼/临界/过阻尼，容差 0.05）→ Localize（仅在 452 个错分测试图上计算逐层梯度范数，取高于中位数的层为"问题层"）→ Diagnose（混淆对→层的错误分类学）→ Treat（冻结 120 个张量，仅解冻 3 个层组 35 个张量，以 α_max=0.01 重训 30 epoch，physics 动量）→ Verify（前/后逐图对比、新错误分析）。跨优化器实验对比 5 种训练方法（基线、Physics、iKFAD、CD、Adam）与 5 种修正策略（层级×2、参数级×2、跨优化器迁移）。
5) 穷举零假设：对 7 个层组的全部 C(7,3)=35 种组合在同一 checkpoint、同一协议下逐一重训，得到完整"层选择景观"分布作为精确零假设；同时对诊断臂做重复跑以分离运行间噪声。
6) 混合配方：Hybrid-90（精度触发：≥90% 即切 μ=0.9）与 Hybrid-ep52（固定 epoch 触发）对比，考察自适应切换 vs 固定切换。

Result: (a) 调度效果：Physics 达 90% 用 47.0±6.4 epoch，基线 107.8±10.1，逐种子加速 2.08/2.86/2.58/2.46/1.71×（均值 2.34×，5/5 种子，Friedman χ²=7.60, p=0.022；配对 t=9.24，单侧 p=4×10⁻⁴），终值 95.02±0.19% vs 基线 95.49±0.08%，即 −0.46pp（5/5 种子，p=0.009，v2 曾误称其"在种子方差内"）；Nesterov 无加速、heavy-ball 更差，说明加速不能由动量变体解释。短调度对照显示 T=60 基线虽 38.7 epoch 到 90% 但仅 94.55%（比 physics 低 0.47pp），T=100 基线 58.3 epoch 到 90% 且精度相同，physics 在等精度下 Pareto 占优，且无额外计算开销（训练时长相同）。阻尼扫描：基线 84.5% 训练处于欠阻尼，physics 67% 处于临界带且从不过阻尼。(b) 泛化：VGG-16(BN) 加速 1.72×（逐种子 1.91/1.80/1.43×，配对 p=0.026），终值 −0.07~−0.61pp；CIFAR-100 上 60%/65% 阈值加速 2.2~2.9×/2.3~3.9×，但终值 −1.70pp，等精度里程碑加速压缩到 1.14×，98% 阈值下 2/3 种子未达标、0.99×，明确划定适用边界。(c) 混合配方：Hybrid-90 在第 47 epoch（首次达 90%）切换，95% 达于第 170 epoch（基线 175、pure physics 183），多种子终值 95.45±0.05%（与基线 95.49±0.08% 不可区分），45.2±9.5 epoch 到 90%（2.4× 加速），切换 epoch 稳定在 35–60；固定 epoch 触发（Hybrid-ep52）因该次运行在第 45 epoch 峰值 89.9% 未站稳 90%、切换本身短暂掉 8pp，导致第 109 epoch 才首次持续达 90%，说明精度触发优于 epoch 触发。(d) 诊断与外科修正：梯度范数集中于 layer3 (10.56)、conv1 (9.14)、layer2 (8.58)，最高/最低比 12.3×；错误可归因到纹理（conv1）、形状（layer2）、中层特征（layer3）；仅解冻 35/120 张量、重训 30/200 epoch（85% epoch 级算力节省）修复 55 个错误、引入 49 个新错误、净 +6。跨优化器不变性成立：SGD 基线与 Adam 模型上梯度归因选出完全相同的 3 层（100% 重合），而 iKFAD 选出 bn1/layer3/layer2（仅 2/3 重合）——说明该信号测的是架构属性而非优化器产物。修正对比：层级修正（A/B 各修 62 个）优于参数级（C 修 42、D 修 46，净 −17/−10）；iKFAD 层因选 bn1 而副作用更少（+22 vs +7）；Adam 跨优化器修正修 146 个但新增 148（净 −2），说明诊断可迁移但修正超参需重调。(e) 穷举负结果（最重要）：35 种 3/7 组合景观跨度 +18 至 −20 净错误；诊断三元组 conv1+layer2+layer3 仅排第 11/35（精确 p=11/35=0.31），与随机无异；Method B 同一组合在标准化协议下由 +22 降为 +9，暴露 v2 结论由运行间变异主导。存活的两个弱信号：含 top 排名层 layer3 的 15 个组合平均 +6.2 净错误 vs 不含的 20 个 −1.4；排名末端的组合可靠地最有害（−13~−20）。作者强调整个景观仅约 ±0.2pp 精度、外科修正目前只是测量工具而非实用技术。

Conclusion: 作者定位自己是"诊断框架而非优化器"：公式来自 Qian (1999)，本文贡献在受控的多种子验证、权衡的诚实量化、Hybrid-90 实用配方，以及一个可复用的组合零假设基线。两条教训：(1) 可立即使用的实践结论——按 μ(t)=1-2√α(t) 训练直至精度接近平台期，再切回 μ=0.9，即得早期 2.4× 加速且终值无损；(2) 方法论教训——v2 关于"梯度归因可选出该修的层"的结论被 35 种组合穷举推翻（仅 11/35，p=0.31），真实信息只存在于排序两端（顶部软正向信号、底部可靠的"不要动"避害信号），因此外科微调与知识编辑类工作的层选择声明必须对照完整组合零假设，而非几次随机挑选的对照。局限：核心结论仅 n=5（泛化臂 n=3）种子、p 值仅作描述性；证据限于 32×32 尺度、SGD+momentum、cosine 调度；CIFAR-100 上纯调度终值代价 −1.7pp（混合配方待验证）；全局 μ 未做逐层/逐模态推广；Adam 下修正超参未调；LLM/Transformer（自适应优化器下动量为 EMA 系数，临界阻尼分析不适用）上的稀疏层信号仍是开放问题，作者据此提出"LLM 错误定位→手术式 LoRA→跨架构不变性"的三级验证计划并公开全部 35 组合景观与原始数据。

Abstract: Standard neural network training uses constant momentum (typically 0.9), a convention dating to 1964 with limited theoretical justification for its
  optimality. We derive a time-varying momentum schedule from the critically damped harmonic oscillator: mu(t) = 1 - 2*sqrt(alpha(t)), where alpha(t) is
  the current learning rate. This beta-schedule requires zero free parameters beyond the existing learning rate schedule. On ResNet-18/CIFAR-10,
  beta-scheduling delivers 1.9x faster convergence to 90% accuracy compared to constant momentum. More importantly, the per-layer gradient attribution
  under this schedule produces a cross-optimizer invariant diagnostic: the same three problem layers are identified regardless of whether the model was
  trained with SGD or Adam (100% overlap). Surgical correction of only these layers fixes 62 misclassifications while retraining only 18% of parameters.
  A hybrid schedule -- physics momentum for fast early convergence, then constant momentum for the final refinement -- reaches 95% accuracy fastest
  among five methods tested. The main contribution is not an accuracy improvement but a principled, parameter-free tool for localizing and correcting
  specific failure modes in trained networks.

</details>


### [7] [A Neural Tension Operator for Curve Subdivision across Constant Curvature Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28937)
*Hassan Ugail,Newton Howard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个"共享学习型张力算子":用单个 140,505 参数的残差网络替代经典插值曲线细分中空间不变的全局张力参数 μ,逐边预测插入角 α_j,再交由 E²/S²/H² 三种几何各自的测地插入公式生成新顶点。三条理论结果(结构性 G¹ 安全界、逐边自适应必要性的启发式论证、基于 Wallner–Dyn 保近性的条件性 C¹ 收敛证书)与实验共同表明:该方法在 240 条留出验证曲线上占据保真–光滑 Pareto 前沿的独立位置,弯曲能与 G¹ 角度粗糙度显著优于所有固定张力与流形提升基线,并在 ISS 轨道地面轨迹这一分布外样本上保持同样趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典插值细分方案(Dyn–Gregory–Levin 四点法、Weissman 六点法)存在两个结构性缺陷:一是张力参数 μ 在空间上不变——同一条控制多边形中,平缓弧段与尖锐拐角需要不同张力,单一全局值无法兼顾;二是几何相关实现重复——Wallner–Dyn 的黎曼近邻框架、球面变体、Hüning–Wallner 的强化球面界、双曲平面版本各自独立,支撑 E²/S²/H² 需要多套实现。同时,几何深度学习与神经算子(FNO、DeepONet)表明,用网络学习"局部内蕴描述符 → 逐边插入角"这类映射,有可能同时解决局部自适应与多几何统一两个问题,并可引入经典方案没有的解析性保证。作者也明确把工作定位为"概念验证"而非全面验证。

Method: 1) 统一的角度参数化:采用 Ahanchaou 等的角度式框架,把三种几何的顶点插入统一为单一插入角 α_j(从边切向到插入方向的有符号角);欧氏用正弦定理+旋转矩阵,球面用球面指数映射与 arctan 长度修正,双曲(Poincaré 圆盘)用 Möbius 加法与 arctanh 修正,并加 0.999 范数钳位。

2) 预测器架构(140,505 参数):输入 7 维内蕴特征 x_j = [δ_{j-1..j+2}/π, e_j/ē, e_{j+1}/ē, κ],其中四个归一化外角构成与经典公式相同四点模板,两个相对边长提供经典规则不具备的局部各向异性信息,κ∈{−1,0,+1} 为几何码;结构为可训练 3×8 几何嵌入 → 拼接后线性投影至 d=128(LayerNorm+GELU)→ 4 个预激活残差块(Dropout 0.05)→ 128→32→1 输出头,末端用 sigmoid 缩放到 (−π/4+0.02, π/4−0.02)。网络逐边独立施加,参数量与多边形规模无关,可直接推广到任意顶点数。

3) 理论部分:定理 1 证明对任意有限权重,输出角严格落在 G¹ 安全区间内,cos|α|>cos(π/4−0.02)≈0.721 使球面/双曲插入公式的分母不退化(双曲另加 e^H≤4.0 运行时钳位);Remark 2 给出逐边自适应必要性的启发式论证(并以固定 μ 网格搜索 oracle 最多仅提升 1.4% 作为经验佐证);定理 2 与推论 3 在条件 A–D 下由 O(h) 提升到 O(h²) 的保近性,经 Wallner–Dyn 传递定理得到 C¹ 极限曲线继承,其中 Lipschitz 常数 L≲2.8 由训练后幂迭代估计得到(仅在 h₀>0.56 时形式成立,实验区间 0.52–0.68 部分超出)。

4) 训练:每几何 400 条合成闭曲线(共 1200 条,12 控制顶点/1000 真值点,欧氏椭圆+Fourier+高阶 Fourier+超椭圆;球面 Lissajous+极坐标 Fourier+扰动大圆;双曲缩放 Fourier+双曲圆+偏置椭圆),按弧长(含闭合段)均匀采样控制点以避免回绕边界偏差;80/20 分层划分得 960 训练/240 验证;损失为对称 Chamfer 保真项 + 外角平方平滑项 + 弧长归一化弯曲能 + 旋转一致性正则(对冻结多边形施加 K=2 随机等距变换);前向包含一步 μ=−0.15 无梯度经典预热(避免 α≈0 与 μ<−0.25 振荡)加两步神经细分;AdamW(β=0.9/0.95,wd=1e−4)、余弦调度+5 epoch 预热、300 epoch、batch 8、梯度裁剪 0.5,采用跨几何 mNN 的两级早停(LR 减半/终止),最佳 checkpoint 在 epoch 200;A100 上约 28 小时,推理约 0.8 ms/层。

5) 评估协议与基线:五类基线(四点 μ=0、六点 μ=−0.25、Wallner–Dyn log-exp 流形提升 4pt/6pt、验证集最优 μ oracle、线性自适应启发式 LAH),指标为 mNN、对称 Hausdorff、弯曲能 E_B、G¹ 代理 max|δ_j|;另做几何嵌入消融、损失项消融、噪声控制多边形鲁棒性、ISS 轨道地面轨迹分布外测试,以及由公式 (9) 反解"有效张力" μ_eff 的机制分析。

Result: 1) 保真–光滑权衡明确成立:E² 上学习器 mNN=0.01636(比最优固定 μ oracle 好 53.6%)、弯曲能 20.9(四点 1477.5,降幅>98%)、G¹ 代理 0.237(全部方法最低);S² 上 mNN=0.04905(+18.5%)、弯曲能 13.8、G¹ 0.231;H² 上 mNN=0.00435(+85.2%)、G¹ 0.160(相对四点降 94.2%)。log-exp 流形提升在三种几何上 mNN 更低(如 H² 0.00338、0.00338 vs 0.00435),但弯曲能高出 1~2 个数量级,即以点态保真换取了光滑性代价。

2) 固定张力被全面压制:四/六点与最优 oracle 在所有指标上被支配,oracle 相对四点最多只改善 1.4%,证明单参数方法天花板极低;LAH 介于两者之间(E² G¹≈1.48),说明仅"线性自适应"不足以获得完整光滑收益,非线性学习自适应是必要的。

3) 噪声鲁棒性:对欧氏验证多边形加 σ∈{0…0.20} 高斯扰动,学习器相对四点的 G¹ 优势仅从 91% 收窄到 85%,退化平缓。

4) 消融:去掉几何信号(No-geom)虽在 100 epoch 时跨几何 mNN 最低,但 H² 的 G¹ 恶化 49%、mNN 恶化 29%,说明几何条件化的作用在于平滑性而非保真;Learned 8 维嵌入与 One-hot 几乎等价,收益来自"存在几何信号"而非嵌入表达力。损失项中去除平滑项 λ_S 破坏最严重(S² 的 G¹ 从 0.296 升至 1.121,mNN 在 H² 恶化 81%),λ_B 次要(球面归一化弯曲 0.046→0.096),λ_E 仅提供早期归纳偏置、不损害最终性能。

5) 分布外(ISS 轨道地面轨迹,球面,16 控制点→512 点):Hausdorff 由 0.00414 升至 0.00699(相对 +69%,但绝对增量 0.00285 < 球面周长的 0.3%),弯曲能 129.6→76.0(−41.4%),G¹ 代理 2.235→0.721(−67.8%),六点基线最差。

6) 机制分析:反解出的有效张力分布显著超出经典区间 [−0.5,0]——E² 上双峰(峰约 −3 与 +1.5,均值 −0.39,标准差 1.99),S² 上均值 +0.32(正值把插入点推到测地弧外侧以抵消经典球面细分的向内塌陷),H² 上集中于约 −3(均值 −0.82,靠近圆盘边界抑制过冲);Proposition 5 指出不存在单一经典 μ 能同时匹配三种几何的均值(跨度 1.14 > 经典区间宽度 0.5),且同一曲线相邻边的 μ_eff 可相差达 4 倍,直接印证 Remark 2。代价方面,神经算子单层约比经典四点多 80× 时间,但 12 顶点 k=4 级约 3.2 ms,仍在交互式实时预算内。

Conclusion: 核心贡献是把"全局手工几何参数"替换为"条件在可训练几何描述符上的共享局部算子",并证明该算子可在 E²/S²/H² 间零架构改动共享,同时保留三种几何各自的测地插入规则;其价值不在点态精度,而在以可量化的保真代价换取显著更低的光滑性(弯曲能与 G¹ 粗糙度),适用于轨道轨迹精化、球面/双曲关键帧插值与交互式曲线设计。作者亦坦承局限:训练与验证全部为程序生成的合成闭曲线,开放曲线、可变顶点数、E²/H² 上的真实场景测试均未做;缺少"离散曲率→μ_j 查表"这一启发式基线,难以严格分离"自适应"与"学习非线性"各自的贡献;收敛理论是条件性的(仅给充分条件,Lipschitz 界靠事后估计,且实验区间 h₀ 的一部分不满足形式条件),H² 上原始弯曲能因使用环境欧氏弦长归一化而失真(应改用 G¹ 代理);等变性正则只约束单步一致性,能否在迭代细分中稳定传播仍未验证。未来方向:更宽模板上的图注意力、扩展到三角网格上的面细分(用带插入角的半边+几何特定指数映射)、以及将该"几何描述符条件化的共享预测器"范式推广到黎曼优化与几何感知视觉等任务。

Abstract: Interpolatory subdivision schemes generate smooth curves from piecewise-linear control polygons by repeatedly inserting new vertices. Classical schemes rely on a single global tension parameter and typically require separate formulations in Euclidean, spherical, and hyperbolic geometries. We introduce a shared learned tension predictor that replaces the global parameter with per-edge insertion angles predicted by a single 140K-parameter network. The network takes local intrinsic features and a trainable geometry embedding as input, and the predicted angles drive geometry-specific insertion operators across all three spaces without architectural modification. A constrained sigmoid output head enforces a structural safety bound, guaranteeing that every inserted vertex lies within a valid angular range for any finite weight configuration. Three theoretical results accompany the method: a structural guarantee of tangent-safe insertions; a heuristic motivation for per-edge adaptivity; and a conditional convergence certificate for continuously differentiable limit curves, subject to an explicit Lipschitz constraint verified post hoc. On 240 held-out validation curves, the learned predictor occupies a distinct position on the fidelity--smoothness Pareto frontier, achieving markedly lower bending energy and angular roughness than all fixed-tension and manifold-lift baselines. Riemannian manifold lifts retain a pointwise-fidelity advantage, which this study quantifies directly. On the out-of-distribution ISS orbital ground-track example, bending energy falls by 41% and angular roughness by 68% with only a modest increase in Hausdorff distance, suggesting that the predictor generalises beyond its synthetic training distribution.

Method: 1. **数据来源**：基于TrialsBank数据库的13,700项临床试验数据，定义运营成功为按计划时间表、招募目标和方案规范完成试验直至数据库锁定

   - 第一阶段：预测中间潜在运营风险因素
   - 第二阶段：将预测的潜在风险整合到下游模型，估计运营成功概率
4. **验证策略**：采用阶段性数据划分防止信息泄漏；使用XGBoost、CatBoost和Explainable Boosting Machines进行基准测试

Result: I-III期试验的样本外F1分数分别为0.93、0.92和0.91。纳入潜在风险驱动因素改善了运营失败的区分能力，独立推理评估下性能保持稳健。

Conclusion: 临床试验运营成功可通过潜在风险感知AI框架进行前瞻性预测，该方法实现了早期风险评估，支持数据驱动的临床开发决策。

Abstract: Clinical trials are characterized by high costs, extended timelines, and substantial operational risk, yet reliable prospective methods for predicting trial success before initiation remain limited. Existing artificial intelligence approaches often focus on isolated metrics or specific development stages and frequently rely on variables unavailable at the trial design phase, limiting real-world applicability. We present a hierarchical latent risk-aware machine learning framework for prospective prediction of clinical trial operational success using a curated subset of TrialsBank, a proprietary AI-ready database developed by Sorintellis, comprising 13,700 trials. Operational success was defined as the ability to initiate, conduct, and complete a clinical trial according to planned timelines, recruitment targets, and protocol specifications through database lock. This approach decomposes operational success prediction into two modeling stages. First, intermediate latent operational risk factors are predicted using more than 180 drug- and trial-level features available before trial initiation. These predicted latent risks are then integrated into a downstream model to estimate the probability of operational success. A staged data-splitting strategy was employed to prevent information leakage, and models were benchmarked using XGBoost, CatBoost, and Explainable Boosting Machines. Across Phase I-III, the framework achieves strong out-of-sample performance, with F1-scores of 0.93, 0.92, and 0.91, respectively. Incorporating latent risk drivers improves discrimination of operational failures, and performance remains robust under independent inference evaluation. These results demonstrate that clinical trial operational success can be prospectively forecasted using a latent risk-aware AI framework, enabling early risk assessment and supporting data-driven clinical development decision-making.

Conclusion: 极线性代数为算子学习提供了新的概念视角，特别适用于谱结构重要且需要并行执行的场景。该框架通过将问题分解为正交特征模态，实现了独立计算管线，天然支持模型并行化而无需人工分区。

Abstract: This work revisits operator learning from a spectral perspective by introducing Polar Linear Algebra, a structured framework based on polar geometry that combines a linear radial component with a periodic angular component. Starting from this formulation, we define the associated operators and analyze their spectral properties. As a proof of feasibility, the framework is evaluated on a canonical benchmark (MNIST). Despite the simplicity of the task, the results demonstrate that polar and fully spectral operators can be trained reliably, and that imposing self-adjoint-inspired spectral constraints improves stability and convergence. Beyond accuracy, the proposed formulation leads to a reduction in parameter count and computational complexity, while providing a more interpretable representation in terms of decoupled spectral modes. By moving from a spatial to a spectral domain, the problem decomposes into orthogonal eigenmodes that can be treated as independent computational pipelines. This structure naturally exposes an additional dimension of model parallelization, complementing existing parallel strategies without relying on ad-hoc partitioning. Overall, the work offers a different conceptual lens for operator learning, particularly suited to problems where spectral structure and parallel execution are central.

</details>


### [10] [\texttt{ReproMIA}: A Comprehensive Analysis of Model Reprogramming for Proactive Membership Inference Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28942)
*Chihan Huang,Huaijin Wang,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReproMIA 将「模型重编程」(model reprogramming) 作为隐私泄漏的主动放大器，统一高效地实施成员推断攻击，在低 FPR 区间显著超越现有 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在关键领域广泛部署，因易记忆训练数据而引发隐私担忧；成员推断攻击(MIA)是审计此类隐私风险的黄金标准。但传统 MIA 范式受两大约束：(1) 影子模型训练的计算成本过高；(2) 在低假阳性率(FPR)约束下性能急剧下降，难以在实际部署中直接落地。因此需要一种能主动、放大隐私泄漏信号且计算开销低的新型攻击范式。

Method: 核心洞察：将模型重编程作为隐私泄漏的「主动信号放大器」。通过在冻结目标模型与下游任务之间插入重编程模块(如线性映射/标签重写)，把隐私相关的隐藏表征主动地「诱导」到易于区分成员与非成员的可分空间，从而主动激发并放大模型内部潜在的记忆化隐私足迹。在此框架下，针对不同架构范式给出专门实例化，涵盖 LLM、扩散模型(Diffusion Models)与分类模型三类；整体为 proactive(主动式)、统一且低开销的 MIA 流程，理论分析与实验共同验证了重编程操作能主动诱导和放大表示中的潜在隐私足迹。

Result: 在十余个基准数据集与多种模型架构上的全面实验表明，ReproMIA 一致且大幅优于现有最优基线，尤其在低 FPR 区间实现性能跃迁：LLM 上相对第二名平均提升 5.25% AUC 与 10.68% TPR@1%FPR；扩散模型上分别提升 3.70% AUC 与 12.40% TPR@1%FPR。

Conclusion: 将模型重编程引入隐私审计是可行且有效的：主动干预模型的表示过程可显著放大微弱的成员泄漏信号，从而在保持低计算成本的前提下，在实际部署所需的高精度低误报区间显著提升攻击可用性，证明「主动放大泄漏」比「被动观测影子模型分数」更适合作为 MIA 的新范式，并为 LLM 与扩散模型等新兴架构的隐私审计提供了统一框架。

Abstract: The pervasive deployment of deep learning models across critical domains has concurrently intensified privacy concerns due to their inherent propensity for data memorization. While Membership Inference Attacks (MIAs) serve as the gold standard for auditing these privacy vulnerabilities, conventional MIA paradigms are increasingly constrained by the prohibitive computational costs of shadow model training and a precipitous performance degradation under low False Positive Rate constraints. To overcome these challenges, we introduce a novel perspective by leveraging the principles of model reprogramming as an active signal amplifier for privacy leakage. Building upon this insight, we present \texttt{ReproMIA}, a unified and efficient proactive framework for membership inference. We rigorously substantiate, both theoretically and empirically, how our methodology proactively induces and magnifies latent privacy footprints embedded within the model's representations. We provide specialized instantiations of \texttt{ReproMIA} across diverse architectural paradigms, including LLMs, Diffusion Models, and Classification Models. Comprehensive experimental evaluations across more than ten benchmarks and a variety of model architectures demonstrate that \texttt{ReproMIA} consistently and substantially outperforms existing state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a transformative leap in performance specifically within low-FPR regimes, such as an average of 5.25\% AUC and 10.68\% TPR@1\%FPR increase over the runner-up for LLMs, as well as 3.70\% and 12.40\% respectively for Diffusion Models.

</details>
